<script>
    var loadDeferredStyles = function () {
        var addStylesNode = document.getElementById("deferred-styles");
        var replacement = document.createElement("div");
        replacement.innerHTML = addStylesNode.textContent;
        document.body.appendChild(replacement)
        addStylesNode.parentElement.removeChild(addStylesNode);
    };
    var raf = requestAnimationFrame || mozRequestAnimationFrame ||
        webkitRequestAnimationFrame || msRequestAnimationFrame;
    if (raf) raf(function () { window.setTimeout(loadDeferredStyles, 0); });
    else window.addEventListener('load', loadDeferredStyles);
    var isMobile = false;
</script>
<script src="https://www.bizportal.co.il/assets/js/jquery-3.7.1.min.js"></script>   
<script src="https://www.bizportal.co.il/assets/js/jQuery.loadScroll.js"></script>
<script src="https://www.bizportal.co.il/assets/jquery-ui-1.14.1/jquery-ui.min.js"></script> 
<script src="https://www.bizportal.co.il/assets/js/aria_dialog.js"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    function SetSearchType(s_type) {
        if (s_type == 1) {
            $("#search_papers_wrap").css("display", "block");
            $("#search_articles_wrap").css("display", "none");
            $("#txt_search_articles").val("");
            $("#txt_search_papers").focus();
        }
        else {
            $("#search_papers_wrap").css("display", "none");
            $("#search_articles_wrap").css("display", "block");
            $("#txt_search_papers").val("");
            $("#txt_search_articles").focus();
        }
    }
    function SetSearchAutoComplete() {
        $.widget("custom.catcomplete", $.ui.autocomplete, {
            _create: function () {
                this._super();
                this.widget().menu("option", "items", "> :not(.ui-autocomplete-category)");
            },
            _renderMenu: function (ul, items) {
                var that = this, currentCategory = "";
                $.each(items, function (index, item) {
                    var li;
                    if (item.category != currentCategory) {
                        ul.append("<li class='ui-autocomplete-category'>" + item.category + "</li>");
                        currentCategory = item.category;
                    }
                    li = that._renderItemData(ul, item);
                });
            },
            _renderItem: function (ul, item) {
                var inner_html = '<div class="div_autocomplete_item">';
                inner_html += '<div class="text_wrap">';
                inner_html += '<div class="name">' + item.label.replace(new RegExp("(?![^&;]+;)(?!<[^<>]*)(" + $.ui.autocomplete.escapeRegex(this.term) + ")(?![^<>]*>)(?![^&;]+;)", "gi"), "<strong>$1</strong>") + '</div>';
                inner_html += '<div class="symbol">' + item.value.replace(new RegExp("(?![^&;]+;)(?!<[^<>]*)(" + $.ui.autocomplete.escapeRegex(this.term) + ")(?![^<>]*>)(?![^&;]+;)", "gi"), "<strong>$1</strong>") + " <span style='color:#dcd9d9'>|</span> " + item.symbol.replace(new RegExp("(?![^&;]+;)(?!<[^<>]*)(" + $.ui.autocomplete.escapeRegex(this.term) + ")(?![^<>]*>)(?![^&;]+;)", "gi"), "<strong>$1</strong>") + '</div>';
                inner_html += '</div>';
                inner_html += '</div>';
                return $('<li></li>').data("item.autocomplete", item).append(inner_html).appendTo(ul);
            }
        });
        var current_papers_search_val = "";
        $("#txt_search_papers").catcomplete({
            delay: 0,
            source: function (request, response) {
                $.ajax({
                    url: global_bizportal_web_base_url + "ajax/papers_auto_suggest.ashx?QueryString=" + escape(request.term),
                    dataType: "json",
                    type: "POST",
                    success: function (data) {
                        current_papers_search_val = request.term;
                        response($.map(data, function (item) {
                            return {
                                label: item.PaperName,
                                category: item.PaperType,
                                link: item.PaperLink,
                                symbol: item.PaperSymbol,
                                value: item.PaperId
                            }
                        }))
                    }
                });
            },
            open: function () {
                $('ul.ui-autocomplete').prepend('<li><div class="autocomplete_list_header"><a href="https://www.bizportal.co.il/list/searchpapers?search=' + current_papers_search_val + '" target="_blank">לכל תוצאות החיפוש <span class="icon-doubleArrow"></span></a></div></li>');
            },
            position: { my: "right top", at: "right bottom", collision: "none" },
            minLength: 3,
            select: function (a, b) {
                window.location.href = b.item.link;
            },
            focus: function (event, ui) {
                this.value = ui.item.label;
                // Prevent the default focus behavior.
                event.preventDefault();
                // or return false;
            }
        });

        var current_articles_search_val = "";
        $("#txt_search_articles").autocomplete({
            source: function (request, response) {
                $.ajax({
                    url: global_bizportal_web_base_url + "ajax/biz_articles_auto_suggest.ashx?QueryString=" + escape(request.term),
                    dataType: "json",
                    type: "POST",
                    success: function (data) {
                        current_articles_search_val = request.term;
                        response($.map(data, function (item) {
                            return {
                                ArtName: item.ArtName,
                                ArtImg: item.ArtImg,
                                ArtLink: item.ArtLink,
                                ArtId: item.ArtId
                            }
                        }))
                    }
                });
            },
            position: { my: "right top", at: "right bottom", collision: "none" },
            minLength: 1,
            select: function (a, b) {
                window.location.href = b.item.ArtLink;
            },
            open: function () {
                $('ul.ui-autocomplete').prepend('<li><div class="autocomplete_list_header"><a href="https://www.bizportal.co.il/list/searcharticles/' + current_articles_search_val + '" target="_blank">לכל תוצאות החיפוש <span class="icon-doubleArrow"></span></a></div></li>');
            }
        }).data("ui-autocomplete")._renderItem = function (ul, item) {
            var inner_html = '<div class="div_autocomplete_item_art">';
            inner_html += '<div class="text_wrap">';
            inner_html += '<div class="img"><img alt="" src="' + item.ArtImg + '"/></div>';
            inner_html += '<div class="name">' + item.ArtName.replace(new RegExp("(?![^&;]+;)(?!<[^<>]*)(" + $.ui.autocomplete.escapeRegex(this.term) + ")(?![^<>]*>)(?![^&;]+;)", "gi"), "<strong>$1</strong>") + '</div>';
            inner_html += '</div>';
            inner_html += '</div>';
            return $('<li></li>').data("item.autocomplete", item).append(inner_html).appendTo(ul);
        };
    }
    
    $(function () {
        $('img.lazy').loadScroll();
        $("nav.menu>ul>li").focusin(function () {
            $(this).addClass("hover");
        }).focusout(function () {
            $(this).removeClass("hover");
        });
        $(window).scroll(function () {
            if ($(this).scrollTop() > 150) {
                $("header").addClass("header_scrolled");
            } else {
                $("header").removeClass("header_scrolled");
            }
        });
        $(window).scroll(function () {
            if ($(this).scrollTop() > 150) {
                $('#back-to-top').fadeIn();
            } else {
                $('#back-to-top').fadeOut();
            }
        });
        $('#back-to-top').click(function () {
            $('body,html').animate({
                scrollTop: 0
            }, 800);
            return false;
        });
        setTimeout(function () {
            $.getScript("https://www.bizportal.co.il/assets/js/accessibility/accessibility.js?v=2");
        }, 1000);

        setTimeout(function () {
            $.getScript("https://www.bizportal.co.il/assets/js/SmoothScroll.min.js");
        }, 500);

    var reloadingTimerCounterBase = 0;
    var reloadingTimeout = 240000;
    function ReloadPage() {
        if (reloadingTimeout != -1) {
            reloadingTimerCounterBase += 1000;
            if (reloadingTimerCounterBase == reloadingTimeout) {
                setTimeout("location.reload();", 500); //== to give browser time for setting cookies	
            }
        }
    }
    var rmvbnr = "0";
    jQuery(document).ready(function () {
        if (reloadingTimeout != -1) {
            setInterval(ReloadPage, 1000);
        }
        $(document).on('scroll mousemove resize', function () {
            reloadingTimerCounterBase = 0;
            setTimeout(function () {
                CheckToSetBottomBnr();
            }, 50);
        });

        if (rmvbnr != "1") {
            if ($(window).width() >= 1900) {
                $.get(global_bizportal_web_base_url + 'ajax/biz_articles_helper.ashx?action=get_art_side_1', function (data) {
                    if (data != "") {
                        $("#art_side_right").html(data);
                        $("#art_side_right").css("display", "block");
                    }
                });
                $.get(global_bizportal_web_base_url + 'ajax/biz_articles_helper.ashx?action=get_art_side_2', function (data) {

            }
        }
    })

    function BizMailingRegistration() {
        var email = $.trim($("#FooterNewEmailInput").val());
        if (email != "") {
            if (email.indexOf("@") > -1 && email.indexOf(".")) {

                if (document.getElementById('FooternewsletterValidate').checked) {
                    try {
                        $.get(global_bizportal_web_base_url + 'ajax/biz_mailing_registration_helper.ashx?action=reg&email=' + email, function (data) {
                            if (data == "1") {
                                alert("הצטרפת בהצלחה לניוזלטר שלנו");
                                $("#FooterNewEmailInput").val("");
                            }
                        });
                    }
                    catch (e) {
                        console.log("ERROR WS: " + e);
                    }
                }
                else {

                    alert("שים לב: חובה לסמן אישור קבלת ניוזלטר");
                    $("#FooternewsletterValidate").focus();
                }
            }
            else {
                alert("שים לב: כתובת הדואר האלקטרוני שהוזנה אינה תקינה");
                $("#FooterNewEmailInput").focus();
            }
        }
        else {
            alert("נא הזן את כתובת הדואר האלקטרוני שלך");
            $("#FooterNewEmailInput").focus();
        }
    }

    var current_most_read_active_tab = 1;
    function SetMostReadTab(tab_id) {
        $("#most_read_t" + current_most_read_active_tab + "_data").css("display", "none");
        $("#most_read_t" + tab_id + "_data").css("display", "block");
        $("#most_read_t" + current_most_read_active_tab).removeClass("active");
        $("#most_read_t" + tab_id).addClass("active");
        current_most_read_active_tab = tab_id;
    }
    $.fn.isInViewport = function () {
        var ViewportPrev = 200;
        var elementTop = ($(this).offset().top - ViewportPrev);
        var elementBottom = elementTop + $(this).outerHeight();
        var viewportTop = $(window).scrollTop();
        var viewportBottom = viewportTop + $(window).height();
        return elementBottom > viewportTop && elementTop < viewportBottom;
    };

        }
    }

    function GoogletagPushBanner(container_id) {
        try {
            googletag.cmd.push(function () { googletag.display(container_id); });
        }
        catch (e) {
            console.log("ERROR WS GoogletagPushBanner: " + container_id);
        }
    }
    function RefreshGoogleAdByID(ad_id) {
        setTimeout(function () {
            var arr_adslots = googletag.pubads().getSlots();
            for (i_adslots = 0; i_adslots < arr_adslots.length; i_adslots++) {
                if (arr_adslots[i_adslots].getSlotId().getDomId() == ad_id) {

                    googletag.cmd.push(function () { googletag.pubads().refresh([arr_adslots[i_adslots]]); });
                }
            }
        }, 30000);
    }
    function SetHiLink(id) {
        $("#main_link_" + id).addClass("hover_top");
    }
    $(function () {
        const hamburger = $('.hamburger');
        const menu = $('.side-menu');
        const menu_overlay = $('.menu_overlay');
        function toggleMenu() {
            const isOpen = hamburger.attr('aria-expanded') === 'true';
            hamburger.attr('aria-expanded', !isOpen);
            hamburger.toggleClass('active');
            menu.toggleClass('open');
            menu_overlay.toggleClass('show');
        }
        hamburger.on('click', toggleMenu);
        menu_overlay.on('click', toggleMenu);
        function toggleSubmenu(button) {
            const parent = button.closest('.has-submenu');
            const submenu = parent.find('.submenu');
            const isOpen = button.attr('aria-expanded') === 'true';

            button.attr('aria-expanded', !isOpen);
            parent.toggleClass('open', !isOpen);
            submenu.attr('aria-hidden', isOpen).slideToggle(200);
        }
        // לחיצה על החץ או על כפתור טקסט
        $('.submenu-toggle, .submenu-toggle-text').on('click', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            toggleSubmenu($(this));
        });
        // ESC סוגר את התפריט
        $(document).on('keydown', function (e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && menu.hasClass('open')) {
                toggleMenu();
                hamburger.focus();
            }
        });
        $('.close-menu').on('click', function () {
            // סוגר את התפריט כמו ההמבורגר
            const isOpen = hamburger.attr('aria-expanded') === 'true';
            if (isOpen) {
                hamburger.attr('aria-expanded', false);
                hamburger.removeClass('active');
                menu.removeClass('open');
                menu_overlay.removeClass('show');
                hamburger.focus();
            }
        });
    });
</script>

        function SearchPapers() {
            var search_val = $.trim($("#choosePaperName").val());

            if (search_val != "") {
                $("#div_data").html("מחפש...");
                $.get(global_bizportal_web_base_url + 'ajax/biz_papers_helper.ashx?action=search_papers&search_val=' + search_val, function (data) {
                    $("#div_data").html(data);
                });
            }
            else {
                alert("נא הזן ערך חיפוש");
            }

        }
        function lettersIndexClickSearchallPapersSearch(letter, obj) {

            });
